{"forms":[{"form":"central simple algebras","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"The complex numbers #92;C form a central simple algebra over themselves, but not over the real numbers #92;R (the centre of #92;C is all of #92;C, not just #92;R). The quaternions #92;mathbbH form a 4-dimensional central simple algebra over #92;R.","type":"example"},{"text":"The concept of central simple algebra over a field K represents a noncommutative analogue to that of extension field over K. In both cases, the object has no nontrivial two-sided ideals and has a distinguished field in its centre, although a central simple algebra need not be commutative and need not have inverses (does not have be a division algebra).","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A finite-dimensional associative algebra over some field K that is a simple algebra and whose centre is exactly K."],"qualifier":"ring theory","topics":["algebra","mathematics","sciences"]}],"synonyms":[{"tags":["initialism"],"word":"CSA"}],"upstream_line":7414278,"upstream_offset":16658767223,"upstream_record_sha256":"1f1f66dbc1091593593de690ede374f9d222ac54274c1457b8e150fcec59db9b","word":"central simple algebra"}

{"etymology_number":"1","forms":[{"form":"polls","tags":["plural"]},{"form":"pol","tags":["alternative"]},{"form":"pole","tags":["alternative"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["A survey of people, usually statistically analyzed to gauge wider public opinion."],"synonyms":[{"word":"survey"}]},{"examples":[{"text":"The student council had a poll to see what people want served in the cafeteria.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A formal vote held in order to ascertain the most popular choice."],"synonyms":[{"word":"vote"},{"word":"election"}]},{"examples":[{"text":"The polls close at 8 p.m.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A polling place (usually as plural, polling places)."]},{"glosses":["The result of the voting, the total number of votes recorded."]},{"glosses":["The head, particularly the scalp or pate upon which hair (normally) grows."],"qualifier":"now rare outside veterinary medicine contexts","synonyms":[{"word":"scalp"}]},{"glosses":["A mass of people, a mob or muster, considered as a head count."],"raw_tags":["in extended senses of the above"]},{"glosses":["The broad or butt end of an axe or a hammer."]},{"glosses":["The pollard or European chub, a kind of fish."]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/pɔl/","tags":["Australia"]},{"ipa":"/pəʊl/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"[pɒʊl]","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/pɒl/"},{"ipa":"/poʊl/","tags":["US"]},{"ipa":"/pol/","tags":["Scotland"]},{"ipa":"/poul/","tags":["Wales"]}],"upstream_line":255571,"upstream_offset":1271423939,"upstream_record_sha256":"9f6d5689de93bdf0aa14218628bbb0b039224c99f5ba8a0edeadf4c3a2d7aa2a","word":"poll"}
{"etymology_number":"1","forms":[{"form":"polls","tags":["present","singular","third-person"]},{"form":"polling","tags":["participle","present"]},{"form":"polled","tags":["participle","past"]},{"form":"polled","tags":["past"]},{"form":"pol","tags":["alternative"]},{"form":"pole","tags":["alternative"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"verb","senses":[{"glosses":["To take, record the votes of (an electorate)."],"tags":["transitive"]},{"glosses":["To solicit mock votes from (a person or group)."],"tags":["transitive"]},{"glosses":["To vote at an election."],"tags":["intransitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"He polled a hundred votes more than his opponent.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To register or deposit, as a vote; to elicit or call forth, as votes or voters."]},{"examples":[{"text":"to poll the hair; to poll wool; to poll grass","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To cut off; to remove by clipping, shearing, etc.; to mow or crop."]},{"glosses":["To cut the hair of (a creature)."],"tags":["transitive"]},{"glosses":["To remove the horns of (an animal)."],"tags":["transitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"to poll a tree","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To remove the top or end of; to clip; to lop."]},{"examples":[{"text":"The network hub polled the department’s computers to determine which ones could still respond.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To (repeatedly) request the status of something (such as a computer or printer on a network)."],"tags":["transitive"],"topics":["communication","communications","computing","engineering","mathematics","natural-sciences","physical-sciences","sciences"]},{"glosses":["To be judged in a poll."],"raw_tags":["with adverb"],"tags":["intransitive"]},{"glosses":["To extort from; to plunder; to strip. Especially in conjunction with pill for emphasis."],"tags":["obsolete"]},{"glosses":["To impose a tax upon."]},{"glosses":["To pay as one's personal tax."]},{"glosses":["To enter, as polls or persons, in a list or register; to enroll, especially for purposes of taxation; to enumerate one by one."]},{"examples":[{"text":"a polled deed","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To cut or shave smooth or even; to cut in a straight line without indentation."],"topics":["law"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/pɔl/","tags":["Australia"]},{"ipa":"/pəʊl/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"[pɒʊl]","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/pɒl/"},{"ipa":"/poʊl/","tags":["US"]},{"ipa":"/pol/","tags":["Scotland"]},{"ipa":"/poul/","tags":["Wales"]}],"upstream_line":255572,"upstream_offset":1271449105,"upstream_record_sha256":"b6e12aece79fc01b5e5869bb403d946e88cce619b2f9051840e777eb57177b31","word":"poll"}
